Spatial Data Science · Speaker Notes489 slides · Indonesian and English presenter scripts

Presenter Notes / Catatan Pembicara

Each entry corresponds to the same numbered slide in the premium HTML presentation. The Indonesian and English paragraphs are written as read-aloud scripts, with the methods and key points aligned to the actual slide content. The data used in the teaching examples are synthetic.

Setiap entri dipasangkan dengan nomor slide yang sama pada presentasi. Gunakan pencarian atau pemilih minggu untuk menemukan catatan. Buku catatan ini dapat dibuka berdampingan dengan slide ketika mengajar.

SLIDE 001 / 489 · Overview

Spatial Data Science with Python · Animated Edition

Open slide

BAHASA INDONESIA — NASKAH BICARA

Selamat datang pada mata kuliah Spatial Data Science with Python untuk program MSc GIS. Dalam empat belas minggu kita akan bergerak dari pertanyaan geografis menuju pengolahan data, pemodelan, evaluasi, dan penyajian hasil melalui GIS.

Praktik dilakukan menggunakan Jupyter atau Google Colab. Setiap contoh mengikuti satu dataset pengajaran yang konsisten; tujuan kita bukan hanya menjalankan kode, tetapi memahami alasan ilmiah di balik setiap langkah.

ENGLISH — PRESENTER SCRIPT

Welcome to Spatial Data Science with Python for the MSc GIS programme. Across fourteen weeks, we move from geographical questions to data preparation, modelling, evaluation, and communication through GIS.

Our practical work uses Jupyter or Google Colab. The same teaching dataset appears throughout the course, allowing us to focus not simply on running code, but on the scientific reasoning behind each operation.

SLIDE 002 / 489 · Overview

Why Spatial Data Science Matters

Open slide

BAHASA INDONESIA — NASKAH BICARA

Pada bagian “Why Spatial Data Science Matters”, kita menghubungkan konsep dasar dengan persoalan geografis yang dapat diamati. Poin utama pada slide mencakup Urban planning and smart cities; Transportation and logistics; Environmental science and ecology.

Kita menghubungkan informasi geografis, kualitas data, model statistik, dan interpretasi secara dapat ditelusuri. Saya ingin kita memahami bukan hanya apa yang dilakukan perangkat lunak, melainkan mengapa metode ini sesuai dengan pertanyaan penelitian.

Untuk menguji pemahaman: Informasi apa yang diperlukan agar hasil analisis tidak ditafsirkan secara keliru?

ENGLISH — PRESENTER SCRIPT

In this section on “Why Spatial Data Science Matters”, we connect the underlying concept to a geographic problem that can be examined. The main elements shown include Urban planning and smart cities; Transportation and logistics; Environmental science and ecology.

We connect geographic information, data quality, statistical models, and interpretation in a traceable workflow. The aim is to understand not just what the software does, but why the method is appropriate for the research question.

To check understanding: What additional evidence would prevent a misleading interpretation?

SLIDE 004 / 489 · Overview

Official Table 4 Structure

Open slide

BAHASA INDONESIA — NASKAH BICARA

Materi ini ditempatkan dalam struktur kurikulum MSc GIS. Komponen yang terlihat meliputi Introduction to Data Science; Concept, Data Analytics, The Need; CLO 1, 3; Understanding Exploratory Data Analysis.

Kita menghubungkan informasi geografis, kualitas data, model statistik, dan interpretasi secara dapat ditelusuri. Keberhasilan belajar dinilai dari kemampuan merumuskan masalah, menjelaskan metode, menjalankan analisis, dan menginterpretasikan hasil.

Pada akhir sesi, mahasiswa seharusnya dapat menjelaskan hasil pekerjaannya menggunakan kalimat sendiri, bukan hanya menunjukkan screenshot kode.

ENGLISH — PRESENTER SCRIPT

This content is positioned within the MSc GIS curriculum. The visible components include Introduction to Data Science; Concept, Data Analytics, The Need; CLO 1, 3; Understanding Exploratory Data Analysis.

We connect geographic information, data quality, statistical models, and interpretation in a traceable workflow. Learning is demonstrated by framing a question, justifying the method, running an analysis, and interpreting the output.

At the end of the session, students should be able to explain their result in their own words rather than simply displaying a screenshot of code.

SLIDE 010 / 489 · Overview

From Real World to Spatial Layers

Open slide

BAHASA INDONESIA — NASKAH BICARA

Kita sedang menelusuri alur kerja dari data menuju informasi. Elemen pada layar mencakup Raster is well suited to continuous surfaces such as temperature, elevation, or reflectance; Vector is well suited to discrete features such as parcels, streets, facilities, or administrative boundaries; A strong analyst must understand geometry, scale, support, and how multiple layers interact in analysis. Jelaskan urutan masukan, proses, serta keluaran agar hubungan antartahap terlihat jelas.

Kita menghubungkan informasi geografis, kualitas data, model statistik, dan interpretasi secara dapat ditelusuri. Pilihan representasi spasial harus sesuai dengan skala dan tujuan analisis.

Pertanyaan untuk mahasiswa: Informasi apa yang diperlukan agar hasil analisis tidak ditafsirkan secara keliru?

ENGLISH — PRESENTER SCRIPT

We are tracing the workflow from spatial data to information. The visible elements include Raster is well suited to continuous surfaces such as temperature, elevation, or reflectance; Vector is well suited to discrete features such as parcels, streets, facilities, or administrative boundaries; A strong analyst must understand geometry, scale, support, and how multiple layers interact in analysis. Describe the input, processing stage, and output so that the relationships are clear.

We connect geographic information, data quality, statistical models, and interpretation in a traceable workflow. Spatial representation needs to match analytical scale and purpose.

For the class: What additional evidence would prevent a misleading interpretation?

SLIDE 011 / 489 · Overview

What Is Geospatial Data Analysis?

Open slide

BAHASA INDONESIA — NASKAH BICARA

Sebelum melangkah ke contoh, mari menyamakan definisi. Istilah yang muncul pada halaman ini antara lain Spatial analysis does not end at mapping coordinates; Context comes from attributes, population, temporal patterns, and domain knowledge; The final product is insight: explanation, prediction, prioritization, or communication for decision support; A conceptual stack: coordinates, POIs, demographics, mobility, time, and analytics output. Setiap istilah harus dihubungkan dengan contoh objek, atribut, atau proses spasial yang nyata.

Kita menghubungkan informasi geografis, kualitas data, model statistik, dan interpretasi secara dapat ditelusuri. Perbedaan definisi akan memengaruhi cara memilih variabel, menyusun tabel, dan menafsirkan hasil.

Silakan berikan satu contoh kapan istilah tersebut digunakan dalam pekerjaan GIS.

ENGLISH — PRESENTER SCRIPT

Before moving to examples, let us agree on the terminology. The visible terms include Spatial analysis does not end at mapping coordinates; Context comes from attributes, population, temporal patterns, and domain knowledge; The final product is insight: explanation, prediction, prioritization, or communication for decision support; A conceptual stack: coordinates, POIs, demographics, mobility, time, and analytics output. Each term should be connected to a concrete spatial object, attribute, or analytical operation.

We connect geographic information, data quality, statistical models, and interpretation in a traceable workflow. Definitions matter because they influence variable selection, table structure, and interpretation.

Please give one practical GIS example where one of these terms becomes important.

SLIDE 016 / 489 · Overview

Spatial Statistical Thinking

Open slide

BAHASA INDONESIA — NASKAH BICARA

Sebelum mempercayai output, kita perlu mengenali sumber kesalahan. Halaman ini menyoroti Spatial datasets are rarely independent and identically distributed; Coordinates, neighbourhoods, covariates, and latent spatial effects often matter simultaneously. Kesalahan pada satuan, join, parameter, atau pembagian data dapat menghasilkan grafik yang tampak meyakinkan tetapi menyesatkan.

Kita menghubungkan informasi geografis, kualitas data, model statistik, dan interpretasi secara dapat ditelusuri. Proses yang aman ialah memeriksa data mentah, hasil antara, dan keluaran akhir secara berurutan.

Untuk melatih berpikir kritis: Informasi apa yang diperlukan agar hasil analisis tidak ditafsirkan secara keliru?

ENGLISH — PRESENTER SCRIPT

Before trusting an output, we need to recognise possible failure modes. This page highlights Spatial datasets are rarely independent and identically distributed; Coordinates, neighbourhoods, covariates, and latent spatial effects often matter simultaneously. Errors in units, joins, parameters, or data splitting can produce attractive but misleading figures.

We connect geographic information, data quality, statistical models, and interpretation in a traceable workflow. A sound process checks the raw inputs, intermediate calculations, and final result in sequence.

A question for critical reflection is: What additional evidence would prevent a misleading interpretation?

SLIDE 043 / 489 · Week 01

Explain the Setup Code

Open slide

BAHASA INDONESIA — NASKAH BICARA

Bagian ini menerjemahkan sintaks Python menjadi langkah analitis. Misalnya, pernyataan yang berkaitan dengan from pathlib import Path → Imports specific tools from pathlib so they can be used in the analysis; import numpy as np → Imports a Python library that provides functions used later in the workflow perlu dipahami melalui tiga pertanyaan: data apa yang masuk, apa yang berubah, dan hasil apa yang keluar.

Kunci analisis terletak pada satuan pengamatan, referensi koordinat, waktu pengukuran, dan asal data. Cara terbaik memeriksa kebenarannya ialah menjalankan satu blok kode, membaca hasil antara, dan membandingkannya dengan tujuan analisis.

Jika ditemukan kesalahan, periksa pesan error, bentuk DataFrame, nama kolom, dan urutan eksekusi sebelum mencoba mengubah model.

ENGLISH — PRESENTER SCRIPT

This section translates Python syntax into analytical reasoning. For statements involving from pathlib import Path → Imports specific tools from pathlib so they can be used in the analysis; import numpy as np → Imports a Python library that provides functions used later in the workflow, ask three questions: what data enter, what is transformed, and what output is produced?

Good analysis starts with the observation unit, coordinate reference system, observation date, and data provenance. A reliable approach is to execute one block, inspect the intermediate result, and compare it with the intended analysis.

If an error appears, inspect the traceback, DataFrame structure, column names, and execution order before changing the model itself.

Main Analysis Code

SLIDE 133 / 489 · Week 04

Mathematical Foundation

Open slide

BAHASA INDONESIA — NASKAH BICARA

Kita sekarang membaca dasar matematis dari metode minggu ini. Persamaan yang ditampilkan adalah Moran’s I = (n / S₀) × [Σ_i Σ_j w_ij z_i z_j] / [Σ_i z_i²]. Mohon pahami arti setiap simbol beserta satuannya sebelum memasukkan rumus ke program.

Pilihan tetangga rook atau queen memengaruhi bobot spasial dan nilai Moran’s I. Contoh hitung sederhana membantu memeriksa apakah arah dan besaran keluaran Python masuk akal.

Perhatikan bahwa ketepatan perhitungan matematika tidak menggantikan kebutuhan validasi data dan asumsi spasial.

ENGLISH — PRESENTER SCRIPT

We now examine the mathematical foundation of this week’s method. The displayed expression is Moran’s I = (n / S₀) × [Σ_i Σ_j w_ij z_i z_j] / [Σ_i z_i²]. Explain each symbol and its units before translating the formula into Python.

Open slide

Open slide

SLIDE 405 / 489 · Week 12

Visualization / Output Code

Open slide

BAHASA INDONESIA — NASKAH BICARA

Kode pada halaman ini memperlihatkan tahap visualisasi dan keluaran. Mulailah dari `actual=reference[:,80:]; predicted=(score>=threshold)[:,80:]` dan jelaskan kebutuhan data serta pustaka yang dipakai.

IoU menguji tumpang tindih kelas target dan lebih informatif daripada akurasi piksel bila latar belakang mendominasi. SLIDE 472 / 489 · Week 14

Common Coding Errors and Fixes

Open slide

BAHASA INDONESIA — NASKAH BICARA

Sebelum mempercayai output, kita perlu mengenali sumber kesalahan. Thank You

Treat every notebook as the beginning of a new experiment: change one assumption, measure its effect, and document what you discover. Thank you.
